# raffclar's work in our tree

## Summary

openblack follows raffclar's tree (see [Following raffclar's tree](refactor-conventions.md#following-raffclars-tree)).
This page measures how much of his work is in ours, against his **current** `master`: every pull request merged into
it since upstream openblack's `a243c6b2`, and one more item for his few commits outside any pull request. Each is
**in**, **partial**, **pending** or **n/a**, with its evidence. The table is
[raffclar-status.tsv](raffclar-status.tsv); the [progress dashboard](dashboard.md#raffclars-work-in-our-tree) draws
the same numbers.

The older measure, the port rows of his fixed target build `0222f9f5`, is kept as history in the
[port audit](port-audit.md); its rows are part of the evidence below.

## The measure

*raffclar's work in our tree = (in + half of partial) / (all − n/a)*, counted in pull requests. Every pull request
counts once, whatever its size.

- **in**: everything the pull request does in the game is in our tree: ported (a commit of ours says "Ported from
  raffclar/openblack PR #N" and took all of it), or ours does the same thing. The evidence names where: a file, a
  function, a test or a commit of ours. Where both trees did the same job and ours was kept, the choice is written in
  the porting conflicts list (`conflicts.md`), and it counts as in. Ported pull requests are not yet checked against
  v1.42; that audit comes after the import.
- **partial**: some of it is in our tree and some not; the evidence says which part is missing. A pull request our
  commits cite as ported, but only in part, is partial.
- **pending**: none of its game behaviour is in our tree yet; the evidence says what is missing. Nothing is counted as
  in without evidence.
- **n/a**: work that is deliberately not taken. Its evidence starts with the reason:
  - online play and services (network play, lobby, hosting, the browser builds), parked until the end (user,
    2026-10-24). A skirmish on one machine against the computer is not online, and is counted;
  - his deviations from the original that we have not chosen (only the user chooses one: see
    [the chosen differences](reconstruction.md#what-is-deliberately-different-chosen-differences));
  - what only a force-feedback device reaches, which is not ported (user, 2026-10-27; the `device` status of the
    [progress files](progress/README.md)); its evidence starts with "device";
  - developer tools and behaviour the original does not have;
  - his inspector and MCP adapter (parked by decision C-1);
  - his own docs pages (ours are this wiki);
  - CI, build tooling and his own check harnesses, formatting-only changes and reverts.

  His renderer switch to SDL3 and slang would be n/a too (our renderer stays on bgfx,
  [Milestones](milestones.md#staying-on-bgfx)), but his `master` has not taken it.

How each was decided: on 2026-10-25 every pull request was re-checked against our tree at joint207, except those
our commits cite as ported and the n/a ones. Each was read from its own change in his tree (the merge's second parent
against its first), and our tree was searched for the same behaviour: by his names, by what it does, and in the
wiki. Our commits' "Ported from raffclar/openblack PR #N" lines were checked for whether they took all of it. The
earlier evidence (the port rows of the [port audit](port-audit.md), the direct-port audit of 2026-10-15 and the
2026-10-17 study of his lanes, whose gain table is [raffclar_uplift.tsv](raffclar_uplift.tsv)) was re-checked, not
copied. No commit of ours carries one of his commits as a cherry-pick: our "cherry picked from commit" lines name
our own branches. `docs/tools/progress_stats.py --raffclar <clone>` adds every new pull request of his master as
pending, "not classified yet", to be classified: until then each counts against the measure.

On 2026-10-28 the pull requests merged since that survey (#914 to #1009, his master at `8ae41c6c`) were classified
against our tree at joint278 the same way, and the pending and partial rows up to #913 were re-checked against our
commits since joint207 (22 rows changed: 5 to in, 10 from pending to partial, the rest with their evidence brought up
to date). Of the 96 new ones, 57 are n/a (most online, parked), 7 in, 17 partial and 15 pending.

On 2026-10-29 the backlog survey of his master at `d763bf44` re-checked 42 rows against our tree at joint292: 14 became
in, 16 n/a (pull requests of tests, testbed scenarios or tooling only, and online ones) and 7 rose from pending to
partial; 4 more had their evidence brought up to date.

### What is worth taking (2026-10-28)

The pieces of his newer pull requests that close one of our open rows, save work on one or fix a fidelity
difference, best first. Each is refitted onto our names and slots and measured as its own commit; none is a clean
cherry-pick, his commits being mixed with his network and inspector code.

|---|---|---|---|
